Tooltip: Hunter's Pressure I Your Black Holes capture creatures +5% faster.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE COMBAT SHARD (ID R9)
The Python Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Hunter's Pressure]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Python Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Viper Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] or higher and Combat Category
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Python Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Hunter's Pressure]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Hunter's Pressure ([[Attributes]]); Your Black Holes capture creatures +5%–50% faster.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

A Python Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Python Shard has the following description:
- These snakes rely on their incredible strength to overpower their victims, wrapping around them with crushing force.

## Tips
- The Ragnarock's ability depends on its [[Strength]], therefore the [[Reforging|Withered]] reforge, [[Hot Potato Book|Hot]] and [[Fuming Potato Book|Fuming Potato Books]], [[The Art of War]] and [[Gemstone|Perfect Jasper Gemstone]] are recommended to fully utilize this item's ability.
  - This item's ability also pairs very well with a [[Golden Dragon Pet]] combined with the [[Chimera]] enchant, increase the [[Strength]] gained.
- It is not very useful to stay out of the battle just to be able to activate this ability. It is much better utilized before the start of fights (such as before an [[Ender Dragon]] or [[End Stone Protector]] spawn) or during phases when an enemy cannot be damaged and cannot damage the player to cancel the ability, for example during Slayers or between phases of [[Magma Boss]] or [[Ashfang]].
  - Namely it can be used during the [[Inferno Demonlord]] boss fight's Demonsplit phase to deal a large amount damage to [[Inferno Demonlord|Quazii]] and [[Inferno Demonlord|Typhoeus]].
  - It can also be used during the [[Voidgloom Seraph]] boss fight's Broken Heart Radiation phase to increase damage right after the phase ends.
- Switching one's held item before the ability activates will cancel the ability. However, switching items after the ability activated will maintain the [[Strength]] bonus.
## Trivia
- Falling into the void does not count as taking damage for the purposes of the ability.

The Raider Sword is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Swords|Sword]] purchased from the [[Melancholic Viking|Joyful Viking]]. It increases in power with mobs killed and wood collections, and earns coins on kill.
## Obtaining
The Raider Sword can be purchased from the [[Melancholic Viking|Joyful Viking]] for 130,000 [[Coins]] once his quest is completed.
## Usage
Sword:
Raider Sword [[Damage]]: +80 [[Strength]]: +50
[Effects]
[Earn +20 Coins from monster kills (Level 10+ only); +1 Damage per 500 kills; Max +35; Kills: 0; +1 Strength Strength per 500 wood; Sums wood collections, max +100; Wood collections: 0]
## Trivia
- The Raider Sword used to be recommended as the best early game weapon. However, nowadays a [[Void Sword]] is a better choice.

# Rare Diamond
Aliases: Reinforced
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones

[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RARE_DIAMOND
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Reinforced reforge when combined with Armor.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Defense; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 8.; RARE REFORGE STONE
Rare Diamond is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Reinforced]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]], granting [[Defense]].
## Obtaining
Rare Diamond has a 0.2% chance to drop when mining [[Diamond|Diamonds]] in the [[Diamond Reserve]] and [[Obsidian Sanctuary]] areas of the [[Deep Caverns]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Rare Diamond can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Reinforced]] reforge, to grant [[Defense]], which scales with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining VIII (8)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Reinforced]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+25 Def ([[Defense]])|+35 Def ([[Defense]])|+50 Def ([[Defense]])|+65 Def ([[Defense]])|+80 Def ([[Defense]])|+110 Def ([[Defense]])|+140 Def ([[Defense]])|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- The [[Reforging|Reinforced]] reforge grants the same [[Defense]] as the [[Reforging|Heavy]] reforge without reducing [[Speed]] or [[Crit Damage]].

The Red Elephant Skin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Red Elephant Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 13, 2021. Being [[Rarity|RARE]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 1,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Red Elephant Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Elephant Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

# Red Gift
Aliases: Red Gifts, Red gifts
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Events, Season of Jerry, Gifts
[Red Gift]
Type: Gift item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Winter Sack]]; [[Events Sack]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RED_GIFT
Tooltip: Click a stranger while holding to gift!; Both players get the rewards!; RARE
A Red Gift is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Gifts|Gift]] obtained from [[Jerry's Workshop]]. A player can give this to another player, allowing both of them to receive rewards.
## Obtaining
### Frozen Treasure
Within the [[Glacial Cave]], players can mine [[Glacial Cave|Frozen Treasure]] to obtain Red Gifts.
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv115]|[[Nutcracker]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv175]|[[Yeti]]|1-2|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv25]|[[Grinch]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%|
|[Lv3]|Frosty ([[Frosty (mob)]])|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|2%|
Damaging a [[Reindrake]] also has a 10% chance to drop a Red Gift.
### Minions
Red Gifts can be obtained from [[Minions]] with the [[Krampus Helmet]] upgrade. This grants a 0.0045% (1 in 22,222) chance to generate one per action.
### Chicken Race
One Red Gift can be obtained from completing the first stage of the [[Chicken Race]].
### Season of Jerry
During the [[Season of Jerry]] event, players can earn Gifts in [[Jerry's Workshop]] of all kinds across waves for [[Defend Jerry's Workshop|defending]] the village.
## Usage
### Gifts
A Red Gift can be given to another player to spawn a present in which that player can open, with both players receiving the item inside. Once opened, the item will drop on the ground and only the 2 players that opened and gifted the present will be able to pick up the items. Red Gifts also have a 50% chance to give the player 1–5 [[North Stars]].
[[Snowman Mask]], [[White Gift Talisman]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] line, [[Snowfin Shard]] and the Professional Gifter perk from [[Doug]] give more rewards from Gifts.

# Red Goblin Egg
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crystal Hollows
[Red Goblin Egg]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: What came first the goblin or the egg?
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Dwarven Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 25,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOBLIN_EGG_RED
Tooltip: What came first the goblin or the egg?; RARE
Red Goblin Egg is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item used to forge the [[Spicy Goblin Omelette]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv89]|[[Creeperlobber]]|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.04%|
|[Lv65]|[[Goblin Flamethrower]]|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.04%|
|[Lv96]|[[Murderlover]]|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.04%|
### Treasure Chests
Red Goblin Eggs can be dropped from chests in the [[Goblin Holdout]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. The chests can either be found in naturally spawning structures like the [[Goblin Queen's Den]] and by [[King Yolkar]] or can be spawned by the player while mining [[Hard Stone]] in the [[Goblin Holdout]].
### [[Frozen Corpses]]
Goblin Eggs can be obtained from opening [[Frozen Corpses]].
|Type|Quantity|Chance Per Roll|
|-|-|-|
|Lapis|[[Red Goblin Egg]] 1 Red Goblin Egg|4.18% (1 in 23.93)|
|Lapis|[[Red Goblin Egg]] 2 Red Goblin Egg|2.09% (1 in 47.86)|
|Lapis|[[Red Goblin Egg]] 4 Red Goblin Egg|1.04% (1 in 95.71)|
## Usage
### Spawning Golden Goblins
Throwing a Red Goblin Egg in [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Glacite Mineshafts]] or [[Glacite Tunnels]] has a 5% (1 in 20 eggs) chance to spawn a [[Golden Goblin]], with a base 3.33% chance to be replaced with [[Diamond Goblin]].
### [[The Forge|Forging]]
#### [[Spicy Goblin Omelette]]
- 1 [[Spicy Goblin Omelette]]
  - [[Red Goblin Egg]] 96 (1 stack plus 32) Red Goblin Egg
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Ruby Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Ruby Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Ruby Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Ruby Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Goblin Omelette]] 96 [[Goblin Egg]]
### Fuel
The Red Goblin Egg can be used as a [[Machine Fuel]], adding 15,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]].

A Red Nose is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Ridiculous]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to any [[Helmets|Helmet]]. It grants [[Health]], [[Defense]] and [[Crit Chance]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chests
The Red Nose can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor I]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Floor I ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]])|Diamond|50,000 [[Coins]]|1.1495% (1 in 87 runs)|1.9668% (1 in 50.8 runs)|180|2|
|Floor I ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]])|Emerald|100,000 [[Coins]]|2.1128% (1 in 47.3 runs)|4.3323% (1 in 23.1 runs)|180|3|
|Floor I ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]])|Obsidian|250,000 [[Coins]]|5.745% (1 in 17.4 runs)|8.0893% (1 in 12.4 runs)|180|3|
|Floor I ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]]) (Master Mode)|Diamond|50,000 [[Coins]]|1.2903% (1 in 77.5 runs)|2.1931% (1 in 45.6 runs)|180|2|

### Boss Collection
A Red Nose can be claimed as a reward from reaching [[Bonzo|Bonzo I (1)]], after defeating [[Bonzo]] 25 times.
## Usage
### Reforging
Red Nose can be combined with a [[Helmets|Helmet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Ridiculous]] reforge, to grant [[Health]], [[Defense]] and [[Crit Chance]], which scale with the Helmet's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XVII (17)]]. A Helmet that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Ridiculous]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Ridiculous Bonus, which allows the player to SNEAK to gain 30 [[Defense]] and 50 [[Mana]] for 5s, but lose 20 [[Crit Chance]] for 20s.

## Bugs
- As of SkyBlock 0.27.2, the description of the Loving Bonus incorrectly suggests it increases the [[Ability Damage]] stat, even though it is actually a Multiplicative (Multiplicative buffs are simple multipliers applied at the end, after flat and additive buffs.) bonus to the player's Base Ability Damage.
## Trivia
- It is implied throughout Scarf's lore that this was a scarf his mother gave him, and that he studied [[Necromancy]] to bring her back.
- [[Reforging|Loving]] is the only armor reforge that increases player's damage directly, as opposed to increasing stats.

Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Grog]]
Buy: 20,000 [[Coins]] 50x Red Thornleaves ([[Red Thornleaf]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RED_THORNLEAF_TEA
Tooltip: Basic Brew; Use this item in place of an Awkward Potion for certain potions.; Added to Resistance potions.; Upon being hit, reflect 5% of the damage back to the attacker.; RARE
Red Thornleaf Tea is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Brews|Brew]].
## Obtaining
The player can purchase a Red Thornleaf Tea for 20,000 [[Coins]] and 50 [[Red Thornleaf|Red Thornleaves]] from [[Grog]].
## Usage
Red Thornleaf Tea can be used with a [[Resistance Potion]] so that upon being hit, it reflects 5% of the damage back at the attacker. It can be used in place of an [[Awkward Potion]] in a [[Brewing Stand]] to take effect.
Alternatively, the player can give [[Shifty]] [[Red Thornleaf Tea]] 1 Red Thornleaf Tea and 1 [[Scarleton Premium]] to obtain their [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]].
### Brewing
|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] Recipe|
|-|-|
|[[Red Thornleaf Tea]] 1x Red Thornleaf Tea 1–3x [[Resistance Potion]]|Recipe: Red Thornleaf Tea|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Red Thornleaf Tea.|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REFINED_MINERAL
Tooltip: Drops rarely from Ores and Dwarven Metals while Mayor Cole's Mining Fiesta is active.; A material so pure it comes pre-refined from its place of origin.; Right-click to view recipes!; RARE
A Refined Mineral is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item obtained by mining [[Ores]] and [[Dwarven Metals]] during the [[Mining Fiesta]] event.
## Obtaining
Refined Minerals have a chance to drop when mining [[Ores]], [[Ores|Pure Ores]] and [[Dwarven Metals]] on Public Islands during a [[Mining Fiesta]], following the formula below and its additive multipliers: Chance=Breaking Power5,000×(1+∑Multipliers)
  Where ∑Multipliers is the sum of the multipliers.
|Multiplier|Type of source|Source|
|-|-|-|
|0.01→0.10|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Refined Luck ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Stoneworm Shard]])|
|0.01→0.10|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Refined Luck ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Stoneworm Shard]]) while a [[Fiesta Flask]] is active.|
|1.00|Consumable ([[Consumables]])|[[Fiesta Flask]]|
|0.02→0.10|Shop Perk ([[Shop Perk (disambiguation)]])|Refined Mind perk from [[Doug]]|
|Ore, Pure Ore, Dwarven Metal|[[Breaking Power]]|Chance per Ore, Pure Ore or Dwarven Metal broken (1 in x Ore, Pure Ore or Dwarven Metal broken) Base|Chance per Ore, Pure Ore or Dwarven Metal broken (1 in x Ore, Pure Ore or Dwarven Metal broken) 1.1 Mult. [[Doug]] or [[Scatha Shard]]|Chance per Ore, Pure Ore or Dwarven Metal broken (1 in x Ore, Pure Ore or Dwarven Metal broken) 1.2 Mult. [[Doug]] and [[Scatha Shard]]|Chance per Ore, Pure Ore or Dwarven Metal broken (1 in x Ore, Pure Ore or Dwarven Metal broken) 2.0 Mult. [[Fiesta Flask]]|Chance per Ore, Pure Ore or Dwarven Metal broken (1 in x Ore, Pure Ore or Dwarven Metal broken) 2.1 Mult. [[Fiesta Flask]] and [[Scatha Shard]]|Chance per Ore, Pure Ore or Dwarven Metal broken (1 in x Ore, Pure Ore or Dwarven Metal broken) 2.3 Mult. [[Fiesta Flask]] and [[Scatha Shard]] and [[Doug]]|

## Usage
### Crafting
Refined Minerals can be used to craft a total of 5 items, requiring a total of [[Refined Mineral]] 33 Refined Minerals.
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Mineral Helmet ([[Mineral Armor]])|None|1x Hardened Diamond Helmet ([[Hardened Diamond Armor]]) [[Refined Mineral]] 5x Refined Mineral|Recipe: Refined Mineral ×5 + [[Hardened Diamond Armor]] → [[Mineral Armor]]|
|Mineral Chestplate ([[Mineral Armor]])|None|1x Hardened Diamond Chestplate ([[Hardened Diamond Armor]]) [[Refined Mineral]] 8x Refined Mineral|Recipe: Refined Mineral ×8 + [[Hardened Diamond Armor]] → [[Mineral Armor]]|
|Mineral Leggings ([[Mineral Armor]])|None|1x Hardened Diamond Leggings ([[Hardened Diamond Armor]]) [[Refined Mineral]] 7x Refined Mineral|Recipe: Refined Mineral ×7 + [[Hardened Diamond Armor]] → [[Mineral Armor]]|
|Mineral Boots ([[Mineral Armor]])|None|1x Hardened Diamond Boots ([[Hardened Diamond Armor]]) [[Refined Mineral]] 4x Refined Mineral|Recipe: Refined Mineral ×4 + [[Hardened Diamond Armor]] → [[Mineral Armor]]|
|[[Mineral Talisman]]|None|[[Refined Mineral]] 9x Refined Mineral|Recipe: Refined Mineral ×9 → [[Mineral Talisman]]|
### Forging
Refined Minerals can also be used to forge a total of 6 items, requiring a total of [[Refined Mineral]] 128 (2 stacks) Refined Minerals.
|Slot|Name|Requirements|Forging Tree|
|-|-|-|-|

## Trivia
- Refined Minerals can be obtained from Gemstone blocks broken through [[Mining Spread]] or [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickaxe Abilities]] such as [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickobulus]] or [[Heart of the Mountain|Sheer Force]].
- Refined Minerals used to be heavily sought after when [[Mineral Armor]] used to affect [[Mithril]] Metals.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REFINED_MITHRIL_PICKAXE
Tooltip: Breaking Power 5; Damage: +50; Mining Speed: +350; Mining Fortune: +10; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 3.; RARE PICKAXE
Tutorial:Avoiding scams
Warning! This item is known to be frequently substituted with a [[Mithril Pickaxe]] with [[Reforging|Refined]] reforge in scams. Please refer to Tutorial:Avoiding scams for further information.
The Refined Mithril Pickaxe is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]] that can be obtained from purchasing from [[Bubu]] in the [[Dwarven Village]].
## Obtaining
The Mithril Pickaxe can be purchased from [[Bubu]] for 1 [[Mithril Pickaxe]] and 2 [[Refined Mithril]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain III (3)]].
## Usage
Pickaxe:
Refined Mithril Pickaxe [[Damage]]: +50 [[Breaking Power]]: +5 [[Mining Speed]]: +350 [[Mining Fortune]]: +10
## Scam
Scammers use the [[Andesite Whetstone]] reforge stone to apply the [[Reforging|Refined]] [[Reforging|reforge]] to a standard [[Mithril Pickaxe]], making the item's appearance and lore incredibly similar to that of their more expensive counterpart. To check if the item you are purchasing is the one that you are after, check the lore and look for the following identifiers:
Reforged [[Mithril Pickaxe]]
- [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] ([[Rarity|RARE]] if [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulated]])
- [[Reforging|Refined]] Bonus
[[Refined Mithril Pickaxe]] Refined Mithril Pickaxe
- [[Rarity|RARE]] ([[Rarity|EPIC]] if [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulated]])
- "This item can be reforged!"
## Trivia
- It has an enchant glint, regardless of whether it is [[Enchantments|enchanted]] or not.

|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Now gives +10 [[Mining Fortune]].|

# Refractive Paint Palette
Aliases: Paint Palette, Reflective Paint Palette
Categories: Rare items, Items, Temp/Using Skill Aliases
[Refractive Paint Palette]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REFRACTIVE_PAINT_PALETTE
Tooltip: Consumable; Tracks the number of Dyes that you obtain throughout the game.; If you obtain 7 unique dyes you can consume this item to permanently gain:; +1% chance to find Dyes +1☯ Carpentry Wisdom +1 Intelligence; Dyes obtained: 0\ 7; Only counts unique dyes!; You may only gain this bonus once!; Like a wise man once said, you can never have enough Magic Find.; RARE
Refractive Paint Palette is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
Refractive Paint Palette can be purchased from the [[Regalia Room]] for 75,000,000 [[Coins]]. As it is a rotating item, it may not be available every [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]].
## Usage
The Refractive Paint Palette tracks the player's lifetime dyes dropped and counts how many unique [[Dyes]] they drop up to 7.
Finding 7 unique [[Dyes]] will allow the player to consume the item and gain a permanent stat bonus of the following:
- +1% chance to find [[Dyes]]
- +1 [[Intelligence]]
- +1 [[Carpentry Wisdom]]
The player may only gain the bonus once.
## Game Message
|Condition|Guaranteed Message|
|-|-|
|Consuming|You mix the 7 colors present on the palette into each other and behold the result. A new color, never seen before and never to be seen again, forms a brief second before being washed away. You feel a little bit more creative.|
## Tips
The following [[Dyes]] are recommended to max out the Refractive Paint Palette, in order of easiest to hardest. Please note that, with the exception of [[Dyes]] acquired from [[Shops]] or [[Ananke Feather|Ananke Feathers]], they should be obtained as [[Vincent]]'s selected 3x [[Dyes|Dye]] as well as a [[Bucket of Dye]] and [[Attributes|Miracle Chance]] attribute. The values will assume all three are used.
|Dyes|Notes|
|-|-|
|★ [[Chocolate Dye]]|Obtained in the Chocolate Shop ([[Chocolate Factory]]) with 40,000,000,000 [[Chocolate]]. For ♲ Ironman ([[Ironman]]), this should be turned into the [[Bucket of Dye]] used for later [[Dyes]].|
|★ [[Pure White Dye]]; ★ [[Pure Black Dye]]|Obtained in the [[Bits Shop]] for 250,000 [[Bits]]. at 4,800 [[Bits]] per Cookie ([[Booster Cookie]]). at 9,120 [[Bits]] per Cookie ([[Booster Cookie]]). at 11,040 [[Bits]] per Cookie ([[Booster Cookie]]).|
|★ [[Holly Dye]]|A 0.0383% (3 in 7843) chance from Red Gifts ([[Red Gift]]) for a mean cost of .; 0.90 quantile (90th percentile, meaning 90% of people getting at least one Dye before spending this much.): .|
|★ [[Archfiend Dye]]|A 0.4595% (3 in 653) chance from [[High Class Archfiend Dice]] for a mean cost of .; 0.90 quantile: . Depending on the price of Ananke Feathers ([[Ananke Feather]]), the [[Nadeshiko Dye]] may be cheaper; they cost compared to [[Archfiend Dye]]'s if both are sold.|
|★ [[Livid Dye]]; ★ [[Necron Dye]]|A 0.0612% (3 in 4902) chance from Master Mode - Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]]) and a 0.1224% (3 in 2451) chance from Master Mode - Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]), respectively. These should generally be acquired while obtaining Catacombs L (50) ([[Catacombs]]). If the [[Secret Dye]] is acquired during either, it is recommended to ignore the [[Archfiend Dye]] due to it being the most expensive.|
|[[Wild Strawberry Dye]]; [[Mango Dye]]|While these [[Dyes]] can be obtained for free while actively making [[Coins]], they will take longer to acquire than any recommended dye listed above. They can replace the [[Livid Dye]] and [[Necron Dye]] at lower [[Catacombs]] levels, or the [[Holly Dye]] and [[Archfiend Dye]] for ♲ Ironman ([[Ironman]]).|
|[[Bingo Blue Dye]]|A viable option for players who actively play Ⓑ Bingo ([[Bingo]]) and accumulated many Bingo Points ([[Bingo]]).|
## Trivia
- The Refractive Paint Palette was created by 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Refraction after becoming a [[Minister Election|Minister]] for [[Aura]].
  - Initially this item was supposed to count rare drops from [[Slayer|Slayers]], but it was changed during development because [[Dyes]] are "more unified system that's more intuitive for the player to track"

[Materials]

Raw Materials to upgrade: 3x [[Glacite Jewel]] 51,200x [[Tungsten]] 51,200x [[Umber]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REINFORCED_CHISEL
Tooltip: Gemstones: [❥]; Can be placed in the Fossil Excavator alongside a Suspicious Scrap to begin the excavation process!; Charges: 18; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 8.; RARE CHISEL
The Reinforced Chisel is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Chisels|Chisel]].
## Obtaining
A Reinforced Chisel can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VIII (8)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Reinforced Chisel]] 1 Reinforced Chisel
  - 1 [[Chisel]] 5 [[Fossil Essence]]
  - 2 [[Refined Tungsten]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Tungsten]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Tungsten]]
  - 2 [[Refined Umber]] 320 [[Enchanted Umber]] 51,200 [[Umber]]
  - 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]] 3 [[Glacite Jewel]]
## Usage
A Reinforced Chisel can be used in the [[Fossil Excavator]] with [[Suspicious Scrap|Suspicious Scraps]] to find [[Fossils]] and other loot.
### Forging
The Reinforced Chisel, along with a variety of other items, can be used to forge a [[Glacite-Plated Chisel]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IX (9)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.
#### Forging Tree
- 1 [[Glacite-Plated Chisel]]
  - [[Reinforced Chisel]] 1 Reinforced Chisel 1 [[Chisel]] 5 [[Fossil Essence]] 2 [[Refined Tungsten]] 320 [[Enchanted Tungsten]] 51,200 [[Tungsten]] 2 [[Refined Umber]] 320 [[Enchanted Umber]] 51,200 [[Umber]] 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]] 3 [[Glacite Jewel]]
  - 8 [[Glacite Amalgamation]] 32 [[Gemstone|Fine Onyx Gemstone]] 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Onyx Gemstone]] 204,800 (3,200 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Onyx Gemstone]] 32 [[Gemstone|Fine Aquamarine Gemstone]] 2,560 [[Gemstone|Flawed Aquamarine Gemstone]] 204,800 [[Gemstone|Rough Aquamarine Gemstone]] 32 [[Gemstone|Fine Citrine Gemstone]] 2,560 [[Gemstone|Flawed Citrine Gemstone]] 204,800 [[Gemstone|Rough Citrine Gemstone]] 32 [[Gemstone|Fine Peridot Gemstone]] 2,560 [[Gemstone|Flawed Peridot Gemstone]] 204,800 [[Gemstone|Rough Peridot Gemstone]] 2,048 (32 stacks) [[Enchanted Glacite]] 327,680 (5,120 stacks) [[Glacite]]
  - 1 [[Mithril Plate]] 1 [[Refined Titanium]] 16 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 2,560 [[Titanium]] 5 [[Refined Mithril]] 800 (12 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Mithril]] 128,000 (2,000 stacks) [[Mithril]] 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]] 1 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]]
  - 1 [[Bejeweled Handle]] 3 [[Glacite Jewel]]
### Gemstones
Applying [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] will give different perks. Each Gemstone type grants a different buff to the Chisel, and is not affected by rarity, unlike all other instances of Gemstone being applied:
|Gemstone Slot|Gemstone Name|Bonus|Value by gemstone tier Rough|Value by gemstone tier Flawed|Value by gemstone tier Fine|Value by gemstone tier Flawless|Value by gemstone tier Perfect|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Gemstone]]|Aquamarine|#% chance to gain an additional Chisel ([[Chisels]]) charge upon starting the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|
|[[Gemstone]]|Citrine|#% chance to highlight a piece of treasure upon starting the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|
|[[Gemstone]]|Onyx|#% chance to spawn an additional piece of treasure upon starting the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|
|[[Gemstone]]|Peridot|Gain +#% Fossil Essence when using the the Fossil Excavator.|30%|40%|50%|60%|100%|

Aliases: Reinforced Huntaxe, Reinforced Huntaxe Cursus

[Upgrades]

Next →: [[Savage Huntaxe - Praedator]]
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CURSUS_FERAE
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Damage: +15; Ability: Absorptio RIGHT CLICK This weapon lets you absorb 15% of the basic combat stats of any weapon.; Ability: Vis Temperata Each strike of this weapon has its damage capped at 25% of the enemy's max Health. This weapon cannot cause a fatal blow.; Fera fugit, cursus sequitur.; This item can be reforged!; RARE AXE
A Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Huntaxes|Huntaxe]] crafted at [[Vinesap|Vinesap V (5)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Vinesap V (5) ([[Vinesap]])|1x [[Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus]] 16x [[Stretching Sticks]] 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Vinesap]]|Recipe: [[Vinesap]] x64 ×3 + [[Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus]] + [[Stretching Sticks]] x16 → Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus|
## Usage
The Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus has two abilities:
- Absorptio allows the Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus to absorb 15% of the basic [[Stats|Combat Stats]] ([[Damage]], [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]], etc) of any weapon.
- Vis Temperata caps the [[Damage]] of the Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus at 25% of the enemy's max health and prevents the user from killing the enemy. This allows one to easily syphon their [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]] with a [[Small Pocket Black Hole|Small]] or [[Medium Pocket Black Hole]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Savage Huntaxe - Praedator]]|Vinesap VII (7) ([[Vinesap]])|[[Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus]] 1x Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus 3x [[Enchanted Vinesap]] 32x [[Stretching Sticks]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Vinesap]] ×3 + Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus + [[Stretching Sticks]] x32 → [[Savage Huntaxe - Praedator]]|
## Trivia
- The text in the Huntaxe's description, "Fera fugit, cursus sequitur." is Latin, and means "The beast flees, the charge follows."
  - The ability name "Vis Temperata" means "Tempered force".

The Revenant Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Essence of Unliving]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
A Revenant Shard can be rarely obtained from defeating Tier III and higher [[Revenant Horror]] bosses.
|Tier|Base Chance|
|-|-|
|Tier III|0.8% (1 in 125.35 kills)|
|Tier IV|1.21% (1 in 82.91 kills)|
|Tier V|1.36% (1 in 73.49 kills)|
### Fusion
A Revenant Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Revenant Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Essence of Unliving]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Essence of Unliving ([[Attributes]]); Gain +1%–10% more [[Undead Essence]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Revenant Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Revenant Shard has the following description:
- Revenants emit an aura of death and decay, sapping the life force of those who dare to confront them.

|Jun 5, 2025|Added Revenant.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Revenant.|
|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Renamed Revenant to Revenant Shard.|

The Rich Chocolate Chunk is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that increases the chance of finding unique [[Chocolate Rabbits]] by 6% and also grants +30 [[Chocolate|Chocolate per Second]]. It can be upgraded into the [[Ganache Chocolate Slab]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Smooth Chocolate Bar can be purchased from the [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Shop]], found in the [[Chocolate Factory]].

Rich Chocolate Chunk

It can be used to purchase its upgraded form, the [[Ganache Chocolate Slab]] from the Chocolate Shop for 1 Rich Chocolate Chunk and 3,000,000,000 [[Chocolate]].

# Rift Prism
Categories: Rare items, Accessories
[Rift Prism]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RIFT_PRISM
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +8; While in your accessory bag, you may join the Rift at no cost.; Talk with Erihann in the Wizard Tower of the Hub to consume this accessory.; X Rift-Transferable X Works while in Accessory Bag!; RARE ACCESSORY
The Rift Prism is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]].
## Obtaining
The Rift Prism can be bid on at [[Barrier Street]]. The top 25 bidders win the item.
Rift Prism can also be obtained as a MAJOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Usage
The Rift Prism allows the player to enter the [[Rift Dimension]] at no cost.
The player can talk to [[Erihann]] to consume their Rift Prism for 200,000 [[Coins]], which grants +11 [[Accessory Power]] and applies the effect of the Rift Prism permanently.
## Trivia
- The Rift Prism is one of the few accessories which cannot be [[Recombobulator 3000|recombobulated]].
  - Imbuing it gives 1 less [[Accessory Power]] than a recombobulated one would.
## History

# Robotron Reflector
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Crystal Hollows
[Robotron Reflector]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Crystal Hollows Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ROBOTRON_REFLECTOR
Tooltip: A key component needed by the Professor to repair Automaton Prime in the Lost Precursor City!; Right-click to view recipes!; RARE
Robotron Reflector is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item and one of the items required to obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv71]|[[Automaton]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|2%|
|[Lv67]|[[Automaton]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
### Treasure Chests
A Robotron Reflector can also be found as an drop from treasure chests within the [[Precursor Remnants]]. It has a 0.34% chance to roll as a drop in common loot chests, and a 0.29% chance to roll as a drop in rare loot chests.
## Usage
### Sapphire Gemstone Crystal
Bringing a Robotron Reflector and the 5 other [[Automaton]] components to [[Professor Robot]] in the [[Lost Precursor City]] rewards the player with the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Precursor Apparatus]]|None|1x [[Electron Transmitter]] 1x [[Superlite Motor]] 1x [[FTX 3070]] [[Robotron Reflector]] 1x Robotron Reflector 1x [[Control Switch]] 1x [[Synthetic Heart]]|Recipe: [[Control Switch]] + [[Electron Transmitter]] + [[FTX 3070]] + Robotron Reflector + [[Superlite Motor]] + [[Synthetic Heart]] → [[Precursor Apparatus]]|
## Trivia
- The Robotron Reflector appears to be a recolored version of the [[Soulflow Pile]].

The Rockmite Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Cavernous Tracker]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Rockmite Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Rockmite]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Rockmite Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

||

[Show More]

### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Rockmite Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Cavernous Tracker]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Cavernous Tracker ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Tracking]] while on Mining Islands.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Rosewater Flask
Aliases: Filled Rosewater Flask, Flask, Rose Water, Rose water, Rosewater
Categories: Rare items, Legendary items, Items, Craftable items, Wild Rose Collection, Aging Items
[Rosewater Flask]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Wild Rose VI (6) ([[Wild Rose]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 400,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ROSEWATER_FLASK
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable: ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum: ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 400,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FILLED_ROSEWATER_FLASK
Tooltip: Consume to grow crops within the Greenhouse by several Growth Stages.; Keep this item in your inventory to increase the bonus up to 4 Growth Stages, at which point the item evolves!; Current Bonus: +0 Growth Stages; Co-op wide 2h cooldown.; RARE
A Rosewater Flask is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that increases the Growth Stage of crops in the [[Greenhouse]]. After reaching 4 bonus Growth Stages, it evolves into a Filled Rosewater Flask.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Wild Rose VI (6) ([[Wild Rose]])|1x [[Bottle of Jyrre]] 32x [[Compacted Wild Rose]]|Recipe: [[Compacted Wild Rose]] x4 ×8 + [[Bottle of Jyrre]] → Rosewater Flask|
## Usage
Upon reaching 4 bonus Growth Stage, a Rosewater Flask evolves into a Filled Rosewater Flask with the following message:
EVOLUTION! Your Rosewater Flask has evolved!
Consuming a Filled Rosewater Flask increases the Growth Stage of crops in the [[Greenhouse]] plot the player is standing in by 4. It also grants a permanent 1 [[Farming Fortune]]. The permanent buff can only be gained 5 times.
It gains 1 Growth Stage every 75 hours, taking 300 hours to reach the maximum buff of 4 bonus Growth Stages. A Rosewater Flask ages twice as fast if placed in a [[Time Pocket]].
After consuming a Rosewater Flask or Filled Rosewater Flask, there is a co-op wide 2 hour cooldown before another one can be used.
## Notes
- Unlike other items, Rosewater Flasks cannot be progressed via [[Catacombs Puzzle Rooms|Oruo]]'s Blessing of Time. However, they still age twice as fast in the [[Time Pocket]].
- [[Cheesebite|Cheesebites]] do not grow past the next rat-spawn stage; a stage 3 [[Cheesebite]] will become a stage 4 [[Cheesebite]] instead of a stage 7 [[Cheesebite]].

|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Rosewater Flask as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

The Rotting Branch is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Toil]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to an [[Axes|Axe]], which grants [[Foraging Wisdom]].
## Obtaining
The Rotting Branch can be purchased from the [[Smithmonger]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Rotting Branch can be combined with an [[Axes|Axe]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Toil]] reforge, to grant [[Foraging Wisdom]], which scales with the Axe's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXV (25)]]. An Axe that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Toil]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.

The Royal Compass is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item which points players to the nearest [[Emissaries|Emissary]] while in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
The Royal Compass can be claimed once player reaches [[Commissions|Commission Milestones I]], which requires at least 5 [[Dwarven Mines]] [[Commissions]] to be completed.
## Usage
When the Royal Compass is used inside the [[Dwarven Mines]], a line of green particles will appear. These particles make a straight line towards the nearest [[Emissaries|Emissary]].
Attempting to use the Royal Compass outside the [[Dwarven Mines]] will result in the following message.
You can't use this here!

The Ruby Drill TX-15 is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drills|Drill]].
## Obtaining
The Ruby Drill TX-15 can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]]. It takes 4 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Ruby Drill TX-15]] 1 Ruby Drill TX-15
  - 6 [[Gemstone|Fine Ruby Gemstone]] 480 (7 stacks plus 32) [[Gemstone|Flawed Ruby Gemstone]] 38,400 (600 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Ruby Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Fuel Canister]] 2 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 320 (5 stacks) [[Enchanted Coal]] 51,200 (800 stacks) [[Coal]]
  - 1 [[Drill Motor]] 10 [[Treasurite]] 1 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 25,600 (400 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]] 3 [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] 480 [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 76,800 (1,200 stacks) [[Redstone Dust]] 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]]
## Usage
Drill:
Ruby Drill TX-15 [[Damage]]: +75 [[Breaking Power]]: +7 [[Mining Speed]]: +150 [[Pristine]]: +0.5 [[Gemstone Fortune]]: +60
[Effects]
[Grants +5% ᠅ Gemstone Powder, and +800 Mining Speed when mining Gemstones.]
The Ruby Drill TX-15 has a base 3,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]], which can be increased through a [[Drills|Fuel Tank]]. Additionally, a [[Drills|Drill Engine]] and [[Drills|Upgrade Module]] can be added to the drill through a [[Drill Anvil]] to grant further bonuses.
When breaking blocks, the Ruby Drill TX-15's [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] will deplete. Most resources will remove 1 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] when mined; [[Gemstone|Aquamarine]], [[Gemstone|Citrine]], [[Gemstone|Onyx]] and [[Gemstone|Peridot]] remove 2 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead; [[Glacite]], [[Umber]] and [[Tungsten]] remove 3 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead. [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] can be regained by adding Fuel to the Drill through a [[Drill Anvil]].
This drill also gives a 5% Extra ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]] which makes it good for powder mining.
### Forging
The Ruby Drill TX-15, along with a variety of other items, can be used to forge a [[Gemstone Drill LT-522]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.
#### Forging Tree
- 1 [[Gemstone Drill LT-522]]
  - [[Ruby Drill TX-15]] 1 Ruby Drill TX-15 6 [[Gemstone|Fine Ruby Gemstone]] 480 [[Gemstone|Flawed Ruby Gemstone]] 38,400 [[Gemstone|Rough Ruby Gemstone]] 1 [[Fuel Canister]] 2 [[Enchanted Coal Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Coal]] 51,200 [[Coal]] 1 [[Drill Motor]] 10 [[Treasurite]] 1 [[Enchanted Iron Block]] 160 [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] 25,600 [[Iron Ingot]] 3 [[Enchanted Redstone Block]] 480 [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] 76,800 [[Redstone Dust]] 1 [[Golden Plate]] 1 [[Refined Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Diamond]] 51,200 [[Diamond]] 2 [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 320 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]] 51,200 [[Gold Ingot]] 5 [[Glacite Jewel]]
  - 3 [[Gemstone Mixture]] 12 [[Gemstone|Fine Jade Gemstone]] 960 (15 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Jade Gemstone]] 76,800 [[Gemstone|Rough Jade Gemstone]] 12 [[Gemstone|Fine Amber Gemstone]] 960 [[Gemstone|Flawed Amber Gemstone]] 76,800 [[Gemstone|Rough Amber Gemstone]] 12 [[Gemstone|Fine Amethyst Gemstone]] 960 [[Gemstone|Flawed Amethyst Gemstone]] 76,800 [[Gemstone|Rough Amethyst Gemstone]] 12 [[Gemstone|Fine Sapphire Gemstone]] 960 [[Gemstone|Flawed Sapphire Gemstone]] 76,800 [[Gemstone|Rough Sapphire Gemstone]] 960 [[Sludge Juice]]
## Trivia
- This is the best and most inexpensive [[Drills|Drill]] for mining [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] with [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]].

[Item Metadata]

Tooltip: Drill Part; +250 Mining Speed +30 Mining Fortune; Put this item in the Drill Engine slot of a Drill at a Drill Mechanic!; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 7.; RARE
Ruby-Polished Drill Engine is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drill Parts|Drill Part]] that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It increases [[Drills]]' [[Mining Speed]] by +250 and [[Mining Fortune]] by +30. It is the upgraded form of [[Titanium-Plated Drill Engine]].
## Obtaining
The Ruby-Polished Drill Engine can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.
### Forging Tree

## Usage
The Ruby-Polished Drill Engine can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to increase its stats by +250 [[Mining Speed]] and +30 [[Mining Fortune]]. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.
### Forging
It is also used in forging the [[Sapphire-Polished Drill Engine]], which requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VIII (8)]] and takes 30 seconds.

# Rusty Anchor
Aliases: Treacherous
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones, Fishing, Events, Season of Jerry
[Rusty Anchor]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])

Applied To: [[Fishing Rods]]
Apply Cost: 100,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Shop]

Buy: 64 (1 stack) [[Hunk of Blue Ice]]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RUSTY_ANCHOR
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Treacherous reforge when combined with a Fishing Rod.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Strength  Sea Creature Chance  Treasure Chance; Treacherous Bonus Earn +20% Ice Essence from TREASURE catches.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; RARE REFORGE STONE
The Rusty Anchor is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Treacherous]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], which grants [[Strength]], [[Treasure Chance]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]].
## Obtaining
The Rusty Anchor can be purchased from [[Terry]] for 64 [[Hunk of Blue Ice]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Rusty Anchor can be applied to [[Fishing Rods]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Treacherous]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]], [[Treasure Chance]] and [[Sea Creature Chance]], which scale with the Fishing Rod's rarity. This has no [[Mining]] requirement.
Additionally, it grants the Treacherous Bonus, which grants +20% [[Ice Essence]] from TREASURE catches.

Tooltip: Drill Part; +400 Mining Speed +50 Mining Fortune; Put this item in the Drill Engine slot of a Drill at a Drill Mechanic!; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 8.; RARE
The Sapphire-Polished Drill Engine is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drill Parts|Drill Part]] that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It increases [[Drills]]' [[Mining Speed]] by +400 and [[Mining Fortune]] by +50. It is the upgraded form of [[Ruby-Polished Drill Engine]].
## Obtaining
The Sapphire-Polished Drill Engine can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VIII (8)]]. It takes 30 seconds to forge.

## Usage
The Sapphire-Polished Drill Engine can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to increase its stats by +400 [[Mining Speed]] and +50 [[Mining Fortune]]. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.
### Forging
It is also used in forging the [[Amber-Polished Drill Engine]], which requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IX (9)]] and takes 30 seconds.

# Saving Grace
Categories: Rare items, Items, Craftable items, Ender Pearl Collection
[Saving Grace]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Ender Pearl IX (9) ([[Ender Pearl]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 20,000 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 2,560x (40 stacks (2,560 items total)) [[Ender Pearl]] 1,152x (18 stacks (1,152 items total)) [[Gold Ingot]] 16x [[Apple]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: Apples ([[Apple]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SAVING_GRACE
Tooltip: Safely teleports you back to your private island when you are about to die. This item will be consumed on use.; RARE
Saving Grace is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item. It requires [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearl IX (9)]] ( 50,000 (781 stacks plus 16) [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]] collected) to craft this item. It safely teleports you to your [[Private Island]] when the player is about to die. The item must be present in the player's inventory for it to work.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Ender Pearl IX (9) ([[Ender Pearl]])|16x [[Enchanted Golden Apple]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]] x16 ×8 + [[Enchanted Golden Apple]] x16 → Saving Grace|
## Usage
### Active ability
If your [[Health]] drops below 0 with the item in your inventory, you'll be warped back to your [[Private Island]], with your full [[Health]] and no [[Death]] penalty. The item will be consumed on use.
This item can be useful if you carry a large number of [[Coins]] around. For example, when you need to carry a lot of [[Coins]] to increase the [[Emerald Blade]]'s stats.
This item's ability is similar to the [[Remnant of the Eye]]'s ability, though the [[Remnant of the Eye]] does not bring you to your [[Private Island]] and only works on [[The End]] Island.
### [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contacts]]
Saving Grace can be given to [[Mort]] in exchange for his [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact.
## Trivia
- This item was commonly used by players and YouTubers doing a hardcore challenge where they cannot die as this item prevents you from dying. However it was risky to use the item as it was glitched and sometimes the player would still die.
- An example of it not activating properly was when the Youtuber TimeDeo and his Co-Op members reached 50 million coins and he decided to prank people and his co-op members by holding 50 mil and jumping into the void while holding a Saving Grace. When he did this the Saving Grace teleported him to the island, but only after he died and lost coins.[1]
## Tips
- If you want not to lose coins upon death, it is far more recommended to use the [[Piggy Bank]] item, which works as an accessory on top of saving your coins upon death.
- It is highly recommended to take this item out of your inventory during [[Dungeons]] as its ability is triggered when you die in Dungeons.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added Saving Grace.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed Saving Grace and [[Remnant of the Eye]] activating simultaneously.|

The Scarf Fragment is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Upgrade Fragments|Upgrade Fragment]] used to upgrade items obtained from [[The Catacombs - Floor II|Floor II]] and [[The Catacombs - Floor III|III]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
Scarf Fragments have a 50% chance of dropping from [[Scarf]] Undeads summoned by [[The Watcher]] between [[The Catacombs - Floor VI|Floor VI]] and [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]]. This is not affected by [[Magic Find]].
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv201-540]|[[Scarf (Watcher)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50% (F6-F7 Only)|

|Adaptive Chestplate ([[Adaptive Armor]])|None|1x Adaptive Chestplate ([[Adaptive Armor]]) [[Scarf Fragment]] 8x Scarf Fragment|Recipe: Scarf Fragment ×8 + [[Adaptive Armor]] → [[Adaptive Armor]]|

|Adaptive Boots ([[Adaptive Armor]])|None|1x Adaptive Boots ([[Adaptive Armor]]) [[Scarf Fragment]] 8x Scarf Fragment|Recipe: Scarf Fragment ×8 + [[Adaptive Armor]] → [[Adaptive Armor]]|

|-|-|-|-|
|[[Scarf's Thesis]]|None|[[Scarf's Studies]] 4x Scarf's Studies|Recipe: Scarf's Studies ×4 → [[Scarf's Thesis]]|

### Salvaging
Scarf's Studies can be salvaged for 15 [[Wither Essence]].
### Rift Transfer
Being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], Scarf's Studies can be transferred into the [[Rift Dimension]]. There, it grants +10s [[Rift Time]]; additionally, it can be used to craft the [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Anti-Bite Scarf]] and [[Warmer Wizard Face]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SCARLETON_PREMIUM
Tooltip: Basic Brew; Use this item in place of an Awkward Potion for certain potions.; Increases the effect of Wounded Potions by 10%.; RARE
Scarleton Premium is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Brews|Brew]].
## Obtaining
The player can purchase a Scarleton Premium for 20,000 [[Coins]] and 50 [[Mushroom Spore|Mushroom Spores]] from [[Ezekiel]].
## Usage
Scarleton Premium increases the effect of [[Wounded Potion|Wounded Potions]] by 10%. It can be used in place of an [[Awkward Potion]] in a [[Brewing Stand]] to take effect.
The player can also give [[Shifty]] 1 [[Red Thornleaf Tea]] and [[Scarleton Premium]] 1 Scarleton Premium to obtain their [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]].

# Scatha-in-a-Bottle
Categories: Rare items, Items, Brewing ingredients, Mixins, Mining
[Scatha-in-a-Bottle]
Type: Mixin ([[Mixins]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Nothing-in-a-Bottle]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SCATHA_IN_A_BOTTLE
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Grants +20 Tracking towards Scathas.; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active Booster Cookie); RARE
Scatha-in-a-Bottle is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]].
## Obtaining
If the player right-clicks a [[Scatha]] while holding [[Nothing-in-a-Bottle]], it becomes a Scatha-in-a-Bottle. This does not require the [[Mob]] to have its [[Health]] lowered.
## Usage
### Brewing
A Scatha-in-a-Bottle can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant the player +20 [[Tracking]] towards [[Scatha|Scathas]]. This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: Scatha-in-a-Bottle → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a God Potion is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.
## Trivia
- The Scatha-in-a-Bottle was added by 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Toadstar0 as part of the [[Minister Election|Minister Elections]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 25, 2026|Added Scatha-in-a-Bottle.|
|Sep 21, 2026|Changed effect from +0.1 [[Pristine]] to +20 [[Tracking]] towards Scathas ([[Scatha]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Added Scatha-in-a-Bottle.|

Tooltip: Accessory Power: +8; Grants +0.75 coins per level of the killed enemy.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; RARE ACCESSORY
The Scavenger Artifact is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that causes all monsters to drop extra [[Coins]] when killed.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Scavenger Ring]] 4x [[Blood-Soaked Coins]] 16x [[Freshly-Minted Coins]]|Recipe: [[Blood-Soaked Coins]] ×4 + [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] x4 ×4 + [[Scavenger Ring]] → Scavenger Artifact|
## Usage
Accessory:
Scavenger Artifact
[Effects]
[Grants 0.75 Coins per level of the killed enemy.]
The Scavenger Artifact causes mobs to drop 0.75 [[Coins]] per level, in addition to any coins dropped without it.
This effect stacks with the [[Scavenger]] enchantment, allowing a player to receive 2.55 [[Coins]] per level with [[Scavenger|Scavenger VI]] and the Accessory.

The Scrappy Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Scrap Chance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Scrappy Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Scrappy]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Scrappy Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Scrappy Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Scrap Chance]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Scrap Chance ([[Attributes]]); Increases your chance of finding a [[Suspicious Scrap]] while mining in the [[Glacite Mineshafts]] by +1%–10%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Scrappy Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Scrappy Shard has the following description:
- Tries everything else in order to avoid a fight but, when pushed to it, it usually wins.

Sell: 35,650 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 8,000x (125 stacks (8,000 items total)) [[Magma Cream]] 4,800x (75 stacks (4,800 items total)) [[Netherrack]] 50x [[Magma Chunk]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SEARING_STONE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Strengthened reforge when combined with Equipment.; Legends say this stone will never cease glowing.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Defense  Strength; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 24.; RARE REFORGE STONE
The Searing Stone is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Strengthened]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Equipment]]. Its recipe is unlocked at [[Magma Cream|Magma Cream VI (6)]]. It grants [[Defense]] and [[Strength]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Magma Cream VI (6) ([[Magma Cream]])|30x [[Enchanted Netherrack]] 50x [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] 50x [[Magma Chunk]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] x10 ×5 + [[Magma Chunk]] x50 + [[Enchanted Netherrack]] x10 ×3 → Searing Stone|
## Usage
### Reforging
Searing Stone can be applied to [[Equipment]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Strengthened]] reforge, to grant [[Strength]] and [[Defense]], which scale with the equipment's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXIV (24)]] and can be done with any equipment, regardless of the piece. Equipment that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Strengthened]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+2 Str ([[Strength]]) +3 Def ([[Defense]])|+3 Str ([[Strength]]) +4 Def ([[Defense]])|+4 Str ([[Strength]]) +5 Def ([[Defense]])|+5 Str ([[Strength]]) +6 Def ([[Defense]])|+6 Str ([[Strength]]) +8 Def ([[Defense]])|+7 Str ([[Strength]]) +10 Def ([[Defense]])|+8 Str ([[Strength]]) +12 Def ([[Defense]])|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|

The Seashine Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Diamond Frog]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Seashine Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Seashine]] with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]].
### Fusion
A Seashine Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Seashine Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Diamond Frog]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Diamond Frog ([[Attributes]]); [[Trophy Frogs]] are +0.5%–5% more likely to be DIAMOND.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Seymour's Special Armor is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Armor]] set, which consists of the Velvet Top Hat, Cashmere Jacket, Satin Trousers, and Oxford Shoes.
## Obtaining
Each piece of Seymour's Special Armor is given to the player by [[Seymour]] as a [[Garden Visitors]] reward for completing his offer in [[The Garden]]. Each piece will have a random color when given to the player.
## Usage
Armor Set:
|Helmet|Chestplate|
|-|-|
|Velvet Top Hat ([[Seymour's Special Armor]]); [[Health]]: +25 [[Defense]]: +10|Cashmere Jacket ([[Seymour's Special Armor]]); [[Health]]: +25 [[Defense]]: +10|
|Leggings|Boots|
|Satin Trousers ([[Seymour's Special Armor]]); [[Health]]: +25 [[Defense]]: +10|Oxford Shoes ([[Seymour's Special Armor]]); [[Health]]: +25 [[Defense]]: +10|
[Total]
[Seymour's Special Armor Health: +100 Defense: +40]
## Trivia
- Contrary to the name, it is not of [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] rarity.
- Due to the armor's unique color mechanic, obtaining pieces with certain colors have a higher value than other pieces for certain players, those can include:
  - Pure gray-scale colors, such as ⁠ #000000 and ⁠ #AAAAAA
  - Pure colors, such as ⁠ #FF0000 and ⁠ #FFFF00
  - Colors that match that of another armor piece, such as [[Superior Dragon Armor|Superior Dragon Chestplate]] (⁠ #F2DF11)
  - Colors that match that of a [[Dyes|Dye]], such as [[Wild Strawberry Dye]] (⁠ #FF43A4)
  - 2 or more pieces of Seymour's Special Armor that share the exact same color, especially when it comes as a full set.

## Trivia
- Because of its Tiered Bonus, its a great armor set for getting [[Experience|Experience Levels]] quickly for experience level consuming activities such as [[Experimentation Table]] or [[Enchantments]].
- This armor set does not affect the experience gains from the [[Experience Bottle]] line of progression, limiting its usability.

# Shiny Prism
Aliases: Glistening
Categories: Rare items, Reforge stones, Craftable items, Glowstone Dust Collection
[Shiny Prism]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Glistening
Applied To: [[Equipment]]
Apply Cost: 100,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXIV (24) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 13,800 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 12,800x (200 stacks (12,800 items total)) [[Glowstone Dust]] 10x [[Gazing Pearl]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 10 Gazing Pearls ([[Gazing Pearl]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SHINY_PRISM
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Glistening reforge when combined with Equipment.; No matter the angle you look at it, you still won't find love.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  True Defense  Intelligence; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 24.; RARE REFORGE STONE
The Shiny Prism is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Glistening]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Equipment]]. Its recipe is unlocked at [[Glowstone Dust|Glowstone Dust VI (6)]]. It grants [[Intelligence]] and [[True Defense]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Glowstone Dust VI (6) ([[Glowstone Dust]])|10x [[Gazing Pearl]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]] x10 ×8 + [[Gazing Pearl]] x10 → Shiny Prism|
## Usage
### Reforging
Shiny Prism can be applied to [[Equipment]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Glistening]] reforge, to grant [[Intelligence]] and [[True Defense]], which scale with the equipment's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXIV (24)]] and can be done with any equipment, regardless of the piece. Equipment that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Glistening]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+5 Int ([[Intelligence]]) +2 T.Def ([[True Defense]])|+6 Int ([[Intelligence]]) +2.5 T.Def ([[True Defense]])|+8 Int ([[Intelligence]]) +3 T.Def ([[True Defense]])|+10 Int ([[Intelligence]]) +3.5 T.Def ([[True Defense]])|+12 Int ([[Intelligence]]) +4 T.Def ([[True Defense]])|+15 Int ([[Intelligence]]) +4.5 T.Def ([[True Defense]])|+18 Int ([[Intelligence]]) +5 T.Def ([[True Defense]])|
|Cost to apply|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|100,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- [[Reforging|Glistening]] beats [[Reforging|Brilliant]] by 1 [[Intelligence]] at [[Rarity|RARE]] and [[Rarity|EPIC]] rarities, and is equal at other rarities. [[Reforging|Brilliant]] also gives [[Speed]] and [[Health]], so it is generally a better reforge for combat.
- If one struggles with surviving against the Slayers, it is recommended to use this [[Reforging|Reforge]] on all equipment for [[Wolf Slayer]] and [[Blaze Slayer]]. However if survival is not the issue, it is better to use [[Reforging|Strengthened]].
  - [[Cayenne]] also grants [[True Defense]], and due to being and [[Enchantments|Enchantment]], not a Reforge, it does not remove the option to use a reforge for Damage. Cayenne however is much more expensive.

# Shiny Token
Aliases: Shiny Shard
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Pages with UIs, The Uprising
[Shiny Token]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SHINY_SHARD
Tooltip: Drops from Shiny Pigs during the Year of the Pig!; Can be spent in the Year of the Pig Shop!; * Soulbound * RARE
A Shiny Token is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
A Shiny Token is a guaranteed drop from [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]].
## Usage
Shiny Tokens can be used to purchase items from [[Piggy]].
## Trivia
- Shiny Tokens were initially added as part of 8[[Ranks|[PIG+++]]] Technoblade's Mayor perks, due to his position as a [[Resistance Leader]] following [[Resistance Movement|The Uprising]].
  - Shiny Tokens later gained more uses when 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] MrFaceplate, 8[[Ranks|[PIG+++]]] Technoblade's father, became a [[Minister Election|Minister]] for [[Candidate Aura|Aura]] to craft the [[Blood God Sigil]] and [[Potato Ring]].

A Shredded Line is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Rod Parts|Rod Part]] that grants +250 [[Damage]] and +50 [[Ferocity]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops

Applying a Shredded Line at [[Roddy]] grants +250 [[Damage]] and +50 [[Ferocity]].
## Trivia
- The Shredded Line replaces the [[Shredder]] [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Weapon]], which when maxed out could have up to only +225 [[Damage]] rather than the +250 [[Damage]] plus the base amount of the rod. However, Shredder, as a Fishing Weapon, could be given combat enchantments.

# Silentdepth Shard
Aliases: Crystal Serendipity, Silentdepth
Categories: Rare items, Shards
[Silentdepth Shard]
Type: Shard ([[Attributes]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Shard ID: R29
Families: Treasure Fish ([[Shard Families]])
Skill: Mining
Category: Water
[Attribute]
Name: Crystal Serendipity ([[Attributes]])
Effect: Treasure Chests you find in the [[Crystal Hollows]] have a +1%–10% chance to double their loot.
Requirement: Hunting X (10) ([[Hunting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Syphonable: ✔ Yes
ID-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes
Chameleon-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes
[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: SILENTDEPTH
Tooltip: Crystal Serendipity I Treasure Chests you find in the Crystal Hollows have a +1% chance to double their loot.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 10.; RARE WATER SHARD (ID R29)
The Silentdepth Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Crystal Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fishing
A Silentdepth Shard can be obtained from fishing in the [[Crystal Hollows]] with [[Wooden Bait]]. There is a 100% (1 in 1 rolls) chance for [[Treasure|GOOD CATCH]] drops to be replaced with one.
### Huntraps
A Silentdepth Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Crystal Hollows]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Silentdepth Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Silentdepth Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Crystal Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Crystal Serendipity ([[Attributes]]); Treasure Chests you find in the [[Crystal Hollows]] have a +1%–10% chance to double their loot.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Silentdepth Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Silentdepth Shard has the following description:
- These fish have developed a powerful bite to break through the rock, searching for mineral-rich algae.
## Tips and Tricks
- Regarding [[Silentdepth Shard]] Silentdepth Shard and [[Thyst Shard]]:
  - Because their hunting methods overlap in the [[Jungle]], it is recommended to place [[Huntraps]] in one of the other 3 quadrants.
  - Reliable bodies of water can be found :
    - in the [[Lost Precursor City]]
    - in the [[Goblin Holdout]] at [[Coordinates|361, 166, 526]].
  - Alternatively, one can use [[Wooden Bait]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]] to guarantee reeling in [[Silentdepth Shard]] Silentdepth Shard(s) as their next [[Treasure|GOOD CATCH]].
## Bugs
- [[Thyst Shard]]’s hunting area remains extended across all 4 quadrants despite its description. Thus, [[Silentdepth Shard]] Silentdepth Shard cannot be hunted specifically from [[Huntraps]].
  - A secure alternative is to use [[Wooden Bait|Wooden Baits]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 5, 2025|Added Silentdepth.|
|Jan 20, 2026|0.24.1 RC|Fixed Crystal Serendipity not working on [[Crystal Hollows]] lock-pickable chests.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Silentdepth.|
|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Renamed Silentdepth to Silentdepth Shard.|
|May 6, 2026|0.24.5|Fixed Crystal Serendipity not working on [[Crystal Hollows]] lock-pickable chests.|

The player can right-click on silkwire to travel across it. The direction in which the player is facing whilst they click with it indicates the direction in which they travel across the wire.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SILKY_LICHEN
Tooltip: Right-click to view recipes!; * Co-op Soulbound * RARE
The Silky Lichen is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item used to craft the [[Silk-Edge Sword]].
## Obtaining
### Woods Race
Silky Lichen can be obtained by talking to [[Gustave]] after beating the [[Woods Race]] in 18 seconds or less. Once unlocked, players can buy it from [[Rusty]] for 100,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Silk-Edge Sword]]|None|1x [[Leaping Sword]] [[Silky Lichen]] 1x Silky Lichen 24x [[Enchanted Fermented Spider Eye]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Fermented Spider Eye]] x6 ×4 + Silky Lichen + [[Leaping Sword]] → [[Silk-Edge Sword]]|
## Trivia
- There was a bug that allowed players to obtain a Silky Lichen instead of the [[Pig's Foot]] in the [[End Race]], but it was patched since then. It was eventually properly added in v0.7.4.
- There was a bug that would grant the player a Silky Lichen instead of a [[Hunter Knife]] from the [[Woods Race]].

Silver Froggles are a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] unlocked at [[Lotus|Lotus IV (4)]]. They allow the player to view [[Wormhole|Wormholes]] and fish in them.
## Obtaining
### Crafting

|Lotus IV (4) ([[Lotus]])|1x [[Froggles]] 8x [[Silver Lotus]] 16x [[Enchanted Lily Pad]]|Recipe: [[Silver Lotus]] x4 ×2 + [[Enchanted Lily Pad]] x8 ×2 + [[Froggles]] → Silver Froggles|

Wearing Silver Froggles allows the player to interact with [[Wormhole|Wormholes]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]] and [[Crimson Isle]]. Fishing in a [[Wormhole]] allows the player to catch [[Trophy Frogs|Reality Hopper]] [[Trophy Frogs]].
## Notes
- Silver Froggles, its predecessor, and all of its upgrades can uniquely have the [[Geometric Oddity]] reforge stone applied.

Silver Hunter Armor can be obtained from [[Odger]] after reaching [[Odger|Adept Trophy Fisher]] rank. Once obtained, it can be purchased from Odger's [[Odger|Lost Rewards Shop]] for 500,000 [[Coins]] per piece.
## Usage

Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty; Gain immunity to Sea Creatures but they are immune to you and you can no longer catch them.; Pieces Required: 2+]
Due to its Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty, players no longer catch [[Sea Creatures]] and cannot do damage to them. However, they also gain immunity to any damage from Sea Creatures. The Tiered Bonus: Odger's Blessing also increases the player's chances of catching Trophy Fish.

- Since Tiered Bonus: Peace Treaty and Tiered Bonus: Odger's Blessing only require 2 pieces to activate, [[Froggles]] and a [[Red Sweater]] can be worn when fishing for [[Trophy Frogs]].
## Trivia
- If players would like to loot share Sea Creatures, they can get the damage requirement and switch to Trophy Hunter Armor in order to not die to them. This is especially noticeable for Boss [[Sea Creatures]], such as [[Thunder]] and [[Lord Jawbus]], since they do a lot of damage.
- Silver Hunter Armor nullifies the effects of [[Sea Creature Chance]], without affecting the stat itself.

A Silver Lotus is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Collections|Collection]] item unlocked at [[Lotus|Lotus III (3)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Lotus III (3) ([[Lotus]])|80x [[Lotus]]|Recipe: [[Lotus]] x16 ×7 → Silver Lotus|
### Fishing
A Silver Lotus can occasionally be fished as a GOOD CATCH or a GREAT CATCH [[Treasure]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]].
### Lotus Atoll Chapters
Completing Chapters II through V for [[Researcher Betty]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]] rewards Silver Lotus.
|Chapter|Reward|
|-|-|
|Chapter II ([[Lotus Atoll]])|[[Silver Lotus]] 5 Silver Lotus|
|Chapter III ([[Lotus Atoll]])|[[Silver Lotus]] 10 Silver Lotus|
|Chapter IV ([[Lotus Atoll]])|[[Silver Lotus]] 20 Silver Lotus|
|Chapter V ([[Lotus Atoll]])|[[Silver Lotus]] 40 Silver Lotus|
### Trophy Frog Rewards
Catching the SILVER tier or higher of all 12 [[Trophy Frogs]] allows the player to claim [[Silver Lotus]] 10 Silver Lotus from [[Researcher Ribery]].
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv292]|[[Frog Prince]]|8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|

|[Lv1]|[[Seashine]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|

|[Lv1]|[[Flipflopper]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|20%|
|[Lv65]|[[GorF]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|5%|
|[Lv1]|[[Lotum]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
|[Lv1]|[[Tewtil]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
|[Lv1]|[[Lotusfish]]|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.1%|
Additionally, successfully following every hop of a [[Puddle Jumper]] rewards the player with an extra [[Silver Lotus]] Silver Lotus.
