Skeleton's Helmet is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] unlocked at [[Bone|Bone VIII (8)]]. While the player is wearing it, it will summon 3 Bone Shields that will surround the player.
It is also part of the secret [[Monster Hunter Armor|Monster Hunter]] / [[Monster Raider Armor|Raider]] set.

The Skeletor Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Deadeye]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Skeletor Shard can be obtained from killing a [[Skeletor]] with enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Skeletor Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Lapis Skeleton Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher
||
||

[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Skeletor Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Deadeye]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Deadeye ([[Attributes]]); Increases damage from ranged weapons by +2.5%–25%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Skeletor Shard has the following description:
- Legends say Skeletor was once a powerful knight, now cursed to guard the Catacombs for eternity, attacking any intruders with relentless fury.
## Trivia
- Despite being a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Skeletor Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

The SkyMart Hyper Vacuum can be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 1 [[SkyMart Turbo Vacuum]] and 500 [[Copper]].
## Usage
Vacuum:
SkyMart Hyper Vacuum [[Damage]]: +200
[Effects]
[Ability: Vacuum HOLD RIGHT CLICK; Deals 200 Damage per second to nearby Pests (Pest (Mob Type)). Ability: Pest Tracker LEFT CLICK; Leads you in the direction of the nearest Pest (Pest (Mob Type)).; Cooldown: 1s]
The SkyMart Hyper Vacuum can be used to capture [[Pests]]. When used near a pest, it will deal 200 [[Damage]] per second. [[Pests (Currency)|Pests]] captured with the SkyMart Hyper Vacuum will be sucked up, and can be exchanged at [[Pesthunter Phillip]] for temporary [[Farming Fortune]] and various goods at his shop.
Left Clicking with the SkyMart Hyper Vacuum will create a trail of particles leading to the nearest Pest.

The Snoozle Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Sleepy Spread]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Snoozle Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Snoozle]] with a [[Critter Capsule]] in the [[Critter Safari]].
### Fusion
A Snoozle Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Snoozle Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Sleepy Spread]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Sleepy Spread ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Mining Spread]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Snoozle Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||

[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Snoozle Shard has the following description:
- Though actually native to forests, Snoozles often end up deep underground as a result of seeking out the quietest place possible before they enter a hibernative state often spanning several years.

# Snoozling
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Snoozling]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SNOOZLING
Tooltip: This sluggish plant takes its sweet time to grow. Every now and then, you'll need to give it a little nudge (or punch) to keep it from dozing off for too long.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; RARE MUTATION
A Snoozling is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Snoozling can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 4 [[Creambloom]], 3 [[Dustgrain]], 3 [[Witherbloom]], 3 [[Duskbloom]], and 3 [[Thornshade]] within 1 block of a 3x3 area of farmland.
While growing, a Snoozling needs water and has 20 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it allows for additional items to be found.
While growing, a Snoozling falls asleep during Growth Stages 5, 10, and 15. It cannot grow until right-clicked to be woken up.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Snoozlings also drop 3,150 (49 stacks plus 14) [[Cactus]], 4,200 (65 stacks plus 40) [[Moonflower]], 4,200 [[Sunflower]], 4,200 [[Sugar Cane]], and +33,600 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Snoozling can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 600,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 300 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Snoozling grants an additional 750 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Snoozlings are used to create a [[PlantBoy Advance]], [[Puffercloud]], [[Stoplight Petal]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Waking up|[CROP] Snoozling: Fine, I'm up, I'm up.|
|Waking up|[CROP] Snoozling: Okay, okay, I'm awake! Happy now?|
|Waking up|[CROP] Snoozling: Alright, Alright. No need to get violent!|
|Waking up|[CROP] Snoozling: Sheesh, can't a plant catch a nap?|
## Tips
- Since a [[Lonelily]] or [[Godseed]] could grow within the 3x3 area and prevent a Snoozling from growing, they should be harvested if they appear.
## Gallery
- Snoozling in the Greenhouse Snoozling in the [[Greenhouse]]
## History

|Dec 5, 2025|Added Snoozling.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Snoozling.|

The Snowfin Shard is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Winter's Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Huntraps
A Snowfin Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Jerry's Workshop]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Snowfin Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Snowfin Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Winter's Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Winter's Serendipity ([[Attributes]]); Increases the odds of finding rare items in Winter Gifts ([[Gifts]]) by +2%–20%.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|3|9|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|4|13|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|4|17|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|5|22|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|6|28|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|8|36|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|12|48|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Snowfin Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

# Soggybud
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Mutations
[Soggybud]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SOGGYBUD
Tooltip: A plant that drinks it all in, then expands like a water balloon.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; RARE MUTATION
A Soggybud is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Soggybud can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 2 [[Melon]] and 2 [[Gloomgourd]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Soggybud needs water and has 10 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it retains watering status by 50%.
A Soggybud can only be watered by taking it from nearby crops.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Soggybuds also drop 1,920 (30 stacks) [[Melon Slice]] and +1,920 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Soggybud can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 60,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 30 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Soggybud grants an additional 750 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Soggybuds are used to create a [[Thunderling]] and [[Turtlellini]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Melon Juice Mixin]]|None|1x [[Glass Bottle]] [[Soggybud]] 2x Soggybud 16x [[Melon Juice]]|Recipe: [[Melon Juice]] x16 + Soggybud x2 + [[Glass Bottle]] → [[Melon Juice Mixin]]|
## Gallery
- Soggybud in the Greenhouse Soggybud in the [[Greenhouse]]
## Tips
- Be careful of growing multiple Soggybuds close to each other. They will absorb water from each other, which can impact growth speed and make them decay before they finish growing.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Soggybud.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Soggybud.|
|Feb 2, 2026|Changed Soggybud spread conditions from 8 [[Melon]] to 2 [[Melon]] and 2 [[Gloomgourd]].|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SOUL_STRING
Tooltip: Rare string that can be used to craft weapons that are infused with the power of Arachne.; Right-click to view recipes!; RARE
Soul String is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item obtained by killing [[Arachne]] in the [[Spider's Den]].
## Obtaining
### Arachne
Soul String is a guaranteed drop from [[Arachne]] if the player deals at least 1 damage to it. The amount of Soul String dropped depends on the damage placement of the player and the level of the boss. [Lv300] [[Arachne]] drops 5-6 if the player's damage placement is 1st-6th, and 1 if the player deals more than 1 damage, + the amount of [[Arachne's Calling|Arachne's Callings]] the player placed. [Lv500] [[Arachne]] drops 30-32 if the player places 1st-6th in damage dealt to it, and 1-3 if the player deals more than 1 damage to it. If the player placed the [[Arachne Crystal]], they receive 12 extra Soul String.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Spider Queen's Stinger]]|None|6x [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] 12x [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] [[Soul String]] 48x Soul String|Recipe: [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] x6 ×2 + Soul String x16 ×3 + [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] x6 → [[Spider Queen's Stinger]]|
|[[Spider Queen's Stinger]]|None|1x [[Dark Queen's Soul]] 1x [[Spider Queen's Stinger]] 60x [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] [[Soul String]] 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) Soul String|Recipe: [[Enchanted Dark Oak Log]] x30 ×2 + Soul String x64 ×3 + [[Dark Queen's Soul]] + [[Spider Queen's Stinger]] → [[Spider Queen's Stinger]]|
|[[Venom's Touch]]|None|10x [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] [[Soul String]] 48x Soul String 120x (1 stack plus 56 (120 in total)) [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Red Mushroom]] x60 ×2 + Soul String x16 ×3 + [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] x10 → [[Venom's Touch]]|
|[[Venom's Touch]]|None|16x [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] [[Soul String]] 48x Soul String 120x [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]] x60 ×2 + Soul String x16 ×3 + [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] x16 → [[Venom's Touch]]|
|[[Venom's Touch]]|None|1x [[Venom's Touch]] 2x [[Dark Queen's Soul]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] [[Soul String]] 192x Soul String|Recipe: [[Enchanted Spider Eye]] x64 ×2 + Soul String x64 ×3 + [[Dark Queen's Soul]] x2 + [[Venom's Touch]] → [[Venom's Touch]]|
|[[Souls Rebound]]|None|10x [[Dark Queen's Soul]] [[Soul String]] 192x Soul String 192x [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Ghast Tear]] x64 ×3 + Soul String x64 ×3 + [[Dark Queen's Soul]] x10 → [[Souls Rebound]]|
|[[Braided Griffin Feather]]|None|160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Griffin Feather]] [[Soul String]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) Soul String|Recipe: [[Griffin Feather]] x32 ×5 + Soul String x64 ×4 → [[Braided Griffin Feather]]|

## Obtaining
The Speed Elixir can be purchased from [[Shifted]] for 5 [[Shy Crux|Shy Cruxes]] and 10,000 [[Motes]].
## Usage
The player can consume a Speed Elixir to grant [[Speed Potion|Speed I]] for 5:00, and is the only way to gain the effect in the [[Rift Dimension]] since [[Potions]] cannot be transferred.

# Spicy Goblin Omelette
Aliases: Red Goblin Omelette, Red Omelette
Categories: Rare items, Items, Tools, Mining, Dwarven Mines, Forged items
[Spicy Goblin Omelette]
Type: Drill Part ([[Drill Parts]])
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: May cause infections
HotM ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) Requirement: Heart of the Mountain V (5) ([[Heart of the Mountain]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Goblin Omelette]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Sell: 20,840 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Red Goblin Egg]] 96x [[Goblin Egg]] 512,000x (8,000 stacks (512,000 items total)) Rough Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GOBLIN_OMELETTE_SPICY
Tooltip: Drill Part; Earn +20% Powder from all sources.; Put this item in the Upgrade Module slot of a Drill at a Drill Mechanic!; May cause infections; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 5.; RARE
Spicy Goblin Omelette is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Drills|Drill]] upgrade module that can be obtained from forging in [[The Forge]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It increases ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]], ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]] and ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] gain from all sources by +20% while held.
## Obtaining
The Spicy Goblin Omelette can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]]. It requires 96 (1 stack plus 32) [[Red Goblin Egg|Red Goblin Eggs]], 1 [[Goblin Omelette]], 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Ruby Gemstone]], and 18 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Spicy Goblin Omelette]] 1 Spicy Goblin Omelette
  - 96 [[Red Goblin Egg]]
  - 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Ruby Gemstone]] 80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Gemstone|Fine Ruby Gemstone]] 6,400 (100 stacks) [[Gemstone|Flawed Ruby Gemstone]] 512,000 (8,000 stacks) [[Gemstone|Rough Ruby Gemstone]]
  - 1 [[Goblin Omelette]] 96 [[Goblin Egg]]
## Usage
The Spicy Goblin Omelette can be applied to any [[Drills|Drill]] via [[Drill Anvil]], to grant it 20% bonus to all Powders (᠅ [[Mithril Powder]], ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]] and ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]]) when held. It can then be removed via the same menu for 9,999 [[Coins]], which gives the item back to the player.
## Trivia
- Spicy Goblin Omelette is the only way for a held item to increase ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] gain.
## History

|Aug 20, 2024|Changed Forge recipe: 99 -> 96 [[Red Goblin Egg]] 0 -> 1 [[Goblin Omelette]]|
|Aug 20, 2024|Reduced Forge time from 20 to 18 hours.|
|Jan 30, 2026|Added Spicy Goblin Omelette to [[Museum]].|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Spicy Goblin Omelette as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Sep 13, 2021|Fixed the ability giving 5 [[Mithril]] rather than [[Mithril Powder]].|
|Unknown Date|Removed extra line in lore.|
|Unknown Date|Fixed the powder drop not being affected by the Powder Buff perk and [[Mithril Golem Pet]].|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Now requires Heart of the Mountain V (5) ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) instead of IV.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Now requires 96 Red Goblin Eggs ([[Red Goblin Egg]]) instead of 99 and 1 [[Goblin Omelette]].|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Instead of granting 20 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] when mining [[Titanium]], the ability now grants grants +20% bonus powder from all sources.|

The Spider Catalyst is a [[Rarity|RARE]] Crafting Material that is an [[Drop Chance|7Extraordinary]] drop from Tier II+ [[Tarantula Broodfather|Tarantula Broodfathers]] when at [[Spider Slayer]] II. It is used to make the [[Tarantula Boots]] and the [[Recluse Fang]].
## Obtaining
### Tarantula Broodfather
The Spider Catalyst can be rarely dropped from Tier II or above [[Tarantula Broodfather|Tarantula Broodfathers]] if the player has reached [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer LVL II (2)]].
Drop Chances:
- Tier II: 1.05%
- Tier III: 2.06%
- Tier IV: 0.62%
- Tier V: 0.18%

|[[Tarantula Boots]]|Spider Slayer IV (4) ([[Spider Slayer]])|[[Spider Catalyst]] 1x Spider Catalyst 1x [[Spider's Boots]] 4x [[Tarantula Silk]] 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]]|Recipe: [[Tarantula Silk]] ×4 + Spider Catalyst + [[Enchanted Iron Ingot]] x64 ×3 + [[Spider's Boots]] → [[Tarantula Boots]]|
|[[Spider Shortbow]]|Spider Slayer V (5) ([[Spider Slayer]])|3x [[Tarantula Web]] [[Spider Catalyst]] 3x Spider Catalyst|Recipe: Spider Catalyst ×3 + [[Tarantula Web]] ×3 → [[Spider Shortbow]]|

# Spider Egg Mixin
Categories: Rare items, Items, Mixins, Brewing ingredients, Brewing, Potions, Slayer
[Spider Egg Mixin]
Type: Mixin
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Bartender]]
Buy: 150,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SPIDER_EGG_MIXIN
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Gain +15 Crit Damage!; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active Booster Cookie); ☠ Requires Spider Slayer 8.; RARE
Spider Egg Mixin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] that can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant it an additional effect for its duration.
## Obtaining
### Purchase
A Spider Egg Mixin can be purchased from the [[Bartender]] for 150,000 [[Coins]] once the player reaches [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer VIII (8)]].
## Usage
### Brewing
A Spider Egg Mixin can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant the player +15 [[Crit Damage]]. This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: Spider Egg Mixin → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a God Potion is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Mar 12, 2021|Added Spider Egg Mixin.|
|Unknown Date|God Potion Mixin effects no longer persist across servers.|
|Mar 18, 2021|Fixed God Potion Mixin effects not persisting across servers.|
|Unknown Date|God Potion Mixin effects do not persist across servers, again.|
|Mar 29, 2021|Fixed God Potion Mixin effects not persisting across servers, again.|
|Jul 22, 2025|0.23.2|Changed the effect of the Spider Egg Mixin to grant +15 [[Crit Damage]].|
Linked from: [[Dodge Potion]], [[Egg (disambiguation)]], [[Mixins]], [[Spider (disambiguation)]], [[Spider Slayer]]

Essence Required; [[Spider Essence]]:

|✪✪|15|45|

## Notes
- As of 23 May 2020 spider boots are glitched while visiting islands and use no mana, but don't have as much propulsion.
- As of 24 March 2020 if you try to jump whilst touching a wall you will lose some vertical distance.
- As of 28 August 2020 you can still use the double jump when doing the dungeons race, even when on the Nothing At All option. It also does not use up mana.
## Trivia
- Combined with the [[Grappling Hook]], you can fly.
  - For best effect, look straight up when double jumping.
  - You can also fly if you use a [[Grappling Hook]] with an [[Aspect of the End]] or [[Aspect of the Void]] as well as an [[Aspect of the End]] with Spider Boots. But keep in mind that if you were to fly with an [[Aspect of the End]]/[[Aspect of the Void]] and Spider Boots it would consume more [[Mana]].
  - Technoblade used this method in his video titled "flying in skyblock" and popularized the method among his fans.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Aug 25, 2026|Double Jump ability is now activated by pressing Jump midair instead of Sneak.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Spider's Boots.|
|Jan 13, 2022|Instead of double tapping spacebar it now functions by pressing your sneak key whilst in the air. No longer take the cumulative fall damage of being in the air, and instead only take fall damage from how far you have fallen since you last used the ability.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Double Jump ability is now activated by pressing Jump midair instead of Sneak.|

## Obtaining
### Dungeon Secrets
Spirit Leaps can be found in Dungeons as [[Dungeon Secrets|Secrets]], in stacks of 5. They can be dropped from secret Bats, found on the ground or in secret chests.
## Usage
Item:
Spirit Leap
[Effects]
[Ability: Spirit Leap RIGHT CLICK; Allows you to teleport to any teammate! Grants 1 second of immunity after teleporting, immunity is cancelled upon dealing damage.; Cooldown: 5s]
Spirit Leaps can be used in [[Dungeons]] to teleport to any teammate.
Upon right clicking with a Spirit Leap in hand, a GUI will open showing the heads of all other players currently in the Dungeon. Upon clicking on one of those heads, the player will be teleported to their location. Dead players and players who have left the Dungeon cannot be teleported to.
Spirit Leaps cannot be used in trap rooms; however, players can teleport to other players in trap rooms.
1 Spirit Leap is consumed on teleport. They have a cooldown of 5 seconds and do not cost [[Mana]]. The [[InfiniLeap™]] works similarly, but is able to be used an infinite amount of times.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Aspiring Leap]]|None|[[Spirit Leap]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Spirit Leap|Recipe: Spirit Leap x16 ×7 → [[Aspiring Leap]]|
## Trivia
- By filling the inventory with other items and leaving a stack of 15 Spirit Leaps, they can be individually picked up from one Secret drop, allowing players to get 5 secrets from 1 Spirit Leap drop.
- If you try to use the Spirit Leap outside dungeons, it shows a secret recipe for the [[Aspiring Leap]].
- Spirit Leap can be used as a [[Dungeon Secrets|Dungeon Secret]] for the [[Secret I Potion]].

# Spring Boots
Categories: Rare items, Armor, Events, Bingo
[Spring Boots]
Type: [[Armor]]
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Merchants: Bingo ([[Bingo (NPC)]])
Buy: 150 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SPRING_BOOTS
Tooltip: Feather Falling XX Increases how high you can fall before taking fall damage by 20 and reduces fall damage by 100%.; Ability: To the Moon! SNEAK Charge your jump by sneaking. The longer you sneak, the higher you will jump!; This item can be reforged!; RARE BOOTS
Spring Boots are [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Boots]] that give the player the ability to jump extremely high. They can be purchased from the [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]] for 150 [[Bingo|Bingo Points]] once the player has reached Ⓑ Bingo Rank I (1).
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Spring Boots can be purchased from the [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]] for 150 [[Bingo|Bingo Points]] once the player has reached Ⓑ Bingo Rank I (1).
## Usage
Boots:
Spring Boots
[Effects]
[Feather Falling XX (Feather Falling); Increases how high you can fall before taking fall damage by 20 and reduces fall damage by 100%. Ability: To the moon! SNEAK; Charge your jump by sneaking. The longer you sneak, the higher you will jump.]
## Trivia
- This is the first (and currently only) item to have [[Feather Falling|Feather Falling XX]] (20).
- The Spring Boots are useful in the [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor 7]] boss room, allowing you to easily get the crystals in phase 1. [[Mage|Mages]] and [[Berserk|Berserkers]] are recommended to use this method as using [[Bonzo's Staff]] or [[Jerry-chine Gun]] would consume a lot of [[Mana]] and [[Vitality]], which is highly server dependent.
- The Spring Boots can be used in [[Dungeon Traps|Trap Room]] to "jump" high and skip the parkour.
- These are one of the only non-cosmetic armor pieces that do not grant any stats.
- Until the [[Artist's Abode]] update, Spring Boots were Co-op Soulbound and could not be sold, auctioned, or traded with anyone other than Co-op members. This presented a very unique problem for Bingo players looking to sell their Spring Boots; meaning the seller would join the buyer's Co-op, purchase the Spring Boots from the [[Bingo]] shop, then trade them to their new Co-op member, usually via a middleman.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 31, 2024|Spring Boots are no longer Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Nov 29, 2021|Added Spring Boots.|
|Dec 1, 2021|Spring Boots become obtainable.|
|Aug 6, 2024|0.20.5|Spring Boots are no longer Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|

# Squash
Categories: Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Squash]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Garden Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 75,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SQUASH
Tooltip: Right-click to view recipes!; RARE
A Squash is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item.
## Obtaining
### Farming
- [[Squash]] Squash has a chance to drop when breaking a mature [[Pumpkin]], [[Melon]], or [[Cocoa Beans]] crop while wearing at least 2 pieces of [[Cropie Armor]], [[Fermento Armor]], or [[Helianthus Armor]].
- Different armor pieces do not count towards each other's Tiered Bonuses.
- The drop chance can be increased with [[Overbloom]].
Pieces: Chance
1/4: 0.00% (0 in ∞ crops broken)
2/4: 0.01% (1 in 10,000 crops broken)
3/4: 0.02% (1 in 5,000 crops broken)
4/4: 0.03% (1 in 3,333.3 crops broken)
### Greenhouse
- [[Squash]] Squash has a chance to drop when breaking any crop or mutation with a Harvestable status inside the [[Greenhouse]] while wearing at least 2 pieces of [[Cropie Armor]].
- Different armor pieces do not count towards each other's Tiered Bonuses.
- The drop chance can be increased with [[Overbloom]].
Pieces: Chance
1/4: 0% (0 in ∞ crops broken)
2/4: 4% (1 in 25.0 crops broken)
3/4: 8% (1 in 12.5 crops broken)
4/4: 12% (1 in 8.3 crops broken)
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Squash Helmet ([[Squash Armor]])|None|1x Cropie Helmet ([[Cropie Armor]]) 8x [[Polished Pumpkin]] [[Squash]] 20x Squash 48x [[Enchanted Melon]]|Recipe: Squash x10 ×2 + [[Polished Pumpkin]] x8 + [[Enchanted Melon]] x24 ×2 + [[Cropie Armor]] → [[Squash Armor]]|
|Squash Chestplate ([[Squash Armor]])|None|1x Cropie Chestplate ([[Cropie Armor]]) 8x [[Polished Pumpkin]] [[Squash]] 20x Squash 30x [[Enchanted Cookie]] 48x [[Enchanted Melon]]|Recipe: Squash x10 ×2 + [[Cropie Armor]] + [[Enchanted Melon]] x24 ×2 + [[Polished Pumpkin]] x8 + [[Enchanted Cookie]] x10 ×3 → [[Squash Armor]]|
|Squash Leggings ([[Squash Armor]])|None|1x Cropie Leggings ([[Cropie Armor]]) 8x [[Polished Pumpkin]] 20x [[Enchanted Cookie]] [[Squash]] 20x Squash 48x [[Enchanted Melon]]|Recipe: Squash x10 ×2 + [[Polished Pumpkin]] x8 + [[Enchanted Melon]] x24 ×2 + [[Cropie Armor]] + [[Enchanted Cookie]] x10 ×2 → [[Squash Armor]]|
|Squash Boots ([[Squash Armor]])|None|1x Cropie Boots ([[Cropie Armor]]) 12x [[Polished Pumpkin]] [[Squash]] 20x Squash|Recipe: Squash x10 ×2 + [[Polished Pumpkin]] x6 ×2 + [[Cropie Armor]] → [[Squash Armor]]|
|[[Squash Ring]]|None|1x [[Cropie Talisman]] [[Squash]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) Squash|Recipe: Squash x16 ×8 + [[Cropie Talisman]] → [[Squash Ring]]|
|[[Turbo Gourd]]|None|2x [[Aggourdian]] 2x [[Gloomgourd]] 2x [[Polished Pumpkin]] [[Squash]] 2x Squash|Recipe: [[Aggourdian]] x2 + [[Gloomgourd]] x2 + Squash x2 + [[Polished Pumpkin]] x2 → [[Turbo Gourd]]|
### Composter
Squash can be used to fill up the [[Composter]]'s Crop Meter by 10k Organic Matter.
It can also be used to purchase [[Composter|Composter Upgrades]] tiers 14 to 19
|Tier|Amount required|
|-|-|
|14|[[Squash]] 3 Squash|
|15|[[Squash]] 6 Squash|
|16|[[Squash]] 12 Squash|
|17|[[Squash]] 24 Squash|
|18|[[Squash]] 48 Squash|
|19|[[Squash]] 96 (1 stack plus 32) Squash|
## Bugs
- Squash does not drop from harvesting [[Greenhouse]] crops if wearing [[Fermento Armor]] or [[Helianthus Armor]].
